// 32 bytes\n66\t  manifestHash: Uint8Array; // 4 bytes - CRC-32 of manifest (roots beacon->manifest->blocks chain)\n67\t  filename: string;\n68\t  mimeType: string;\n69\t}\n70\t\n71\t/**\n72\t * Validation error with code and details.\n73\t */\n74\texport class BeaconValidationError extends Error {\n75\t  constructor(\n76\t    public code: string,\n77\t    message: string,\n78\t    public details: Record<string, unknown>\n79\t  ) {\n80\t    super(message);\n81\t    this.name = 'BeaconValidationError';\n82\t  }\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\t/**\n86\t * Maximum safe values for beacon fields (T1).\n87\t *\n88\t * **Beacon size bound calculation (R1's 256-byte payload):**\n89\t * - Fixed fields: 64 bytes (streamId, wireVersion, originalSize, payloadLen, blockSize,\n90\t *                  blockCount, fragmentLen, degreeCap, flags, blockHashLen,\n91\t *                  wholeFileHash, manifestHash)\n92\t * - CRC-32: 4 bytes\n93\t * - Length prefixes: 2 bytes (1 byte each for filename and mimeType)\n94\t * - Available for filename + mimeType: 256 - 64 - 4 - 2 = 186 bytes\n95\t *\n96\t * This yields a conservative allocation of 128 bytes for filename and 58 bytes for\n97\t * mimeType, ensuring the beacon never overflows R1's capacity while accommodating\n98\t * common filenames and MIME types.\n99\t */\n100\texport const BEACON_LIMITS = {\n101\t  MAX_L: 4096,\n102\t  MAX_BLOCK_COUNT: 16_700_000, // 16.7M blocks × 192 KB = 3.0 TB\n103\t  MAX_FILE_SIZE: 281_474_976_710_656, // 281 TB, 6-byte field max\n104\t  /** Maximum number of blocks that the manifest itself can consume (T1). */\n105\t  /** K_manifest = ceil(blockCount × blockHashLen / BLOCK). Limits DoS from unbounded manifest growth. */\n106\t  MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS: 1000, // 1000 manifest blocks ≈ 195 MB of manifest data\n107\t  /** Maximum UTF-8 encoded filename length in bytes (T2). Chosen to fit within R1's 256-byte payload. */\n108\t  MAX_FILENAME_BYTES: 128,\n109\t  /** Maximum UTF-8 encoded MIME type length in bytes (T2). Chosen to fit within R1's 256-byte payload. */\n110\t  MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES: 58,\n111\t  /** Maximum UTF-8 codepoint count for filename (T2). A UTF-8 string can use up to 4 bytes per codepoint. */\n112\t  MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS: 32, // 32 × 4 = 128, guarantees never exceeds MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n113\t  /** Maximum UTF-8 codepoint count for MIME type (T2). */\n114\t  MAX_MIMETYPE_CODEPOINTS: 14, // 14 × 4 = 56, fits within MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES\n115\t} as const;\n116\t\n117\t/**\n118\t * Calculate K_manifest from blockCount and blockHashLen.\n119\t *\n120\t * K_manifest is the number of blocks needed to store the block hash manifest.\n121\t * The manifest contains blockCount × blockHashLen bytes (all block hashes),\n122\t * divided into blocks of size BLOCK (196608 bytes = K × L).\n123\t *\n124\t * Formula: K_manifest = ceil(blockCount × blockHashLen / BLOCK)\n125\t *\n126\t * @param blockCount - Number of data blocks (from beacon)\n127\t * @param blockHashLen - Length of each block hash in bytes (from beacon)\n128\t * @returns Number of blocks needed to store the manifest\n129\t */\n130\tfunction calculateKManifest(blockCount: number, blockHashLen: number): number {\n131\t  const BLOCK = 196608; // K × L where K=768, L=256 (from core/params.ts)\n132\t  const manifestBytes = blockCount * blockHashLen;\n133\t  return Math.ceil(manifestBytes / BLOCK);\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\t/**\n137\t * Device context for K validation logging.\n138\t */\n139\texport interface DeviceContext {\n140\t  /** Device signature from GE benchmark cache */\n141\t  deviceSignature: string;\n142\t  /** User agent string */\n143\t  userAgent: string;\n144\t  /** Platform identifier */\n145\t  platform: string;\n146\t}\n147\t\n148\t/**\n149\t * Parse a beacon from bytes.\n150\t *\n151\t * Beacon format: [body fields...][crc32(4)]\n152\t * The CRC-32 covers all beacon body fields and MUST be validated before\n153\t * any metadata (including streamId) is trusted.\n154\t *\n155\t * **Validation order is critical:**\n156\t * 1. Read all fields (no validation yet)\n157\t * 2. Validate CRC-32 (if this fails, ALL other values are suspect)\n158\t * 3. Then do bounds/K/quota checks (safe now because CRC validated)\n159\t *\n160\t * @param bytes - Beacon payload bytes (after QR decoding and header stripping)\n161\t * @param localKMax - This device's benchmarked maximum K (from GE benchmark)\n162\t * @param availableQuota - Available storage quota in bytes (for T1 originalSize check)\n163\t * @param deviceContext - Optional device context for K validation logging\n164\t * @throws {BeaconValidationError} If any field fails validation or CRC-32 mismatch\n165\t */\n166\texport function parseBeacon(\n167\t  bytes: Uint8Array,\n168\t  localKMax: number,\n169\t  availableQuota: number,\n170\t  deviceContext?: DeviceContext\n171\t): BeaconMeta {\n172\t  let offset = 0;\n173\t\n174\t  const readU32 = (): number => {\n175\t    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 24) |\n176\t                 (bytes[offset + 1]! << 16) |\n177\t                 (bytes[offset + 2]! << 8) |\n178\t                 bytes[offset + 3]!;\n179\t    offset += 4;\n180\t    return value >>> 0; // Force unsigned\n181\t  };\n182\t\n183\t  const readU24 = (): number => {\n184\t    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 16) |\n185\t                 (bytes[offset + 1]! << 8) |\n186\t                 bytes[offset + 2]!;\n187\t    offset += 3;\n188\t    return value >>> 0;\n189\t  };\n190\t\n191\t  const readU16 = (): number => {\n192\t    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 8) | bytes[offset + 1]!;\n193\t    offset += 2;\n194\t    return value >>> 0;\n195\t  };\n196\t\n197\t  const readU8 = (): number => {\n198\t    const value = bytes[offset]!;\n199\t    offset += 1;\n200\t    return value;\n201\t  };\n202\t\n203\t  const readBytes = (n: number): Uint8Array => {\n204\t    const value = bytes.subarray(offset, offset + n);\n205\t    offset += n;\n206\t    return value;\n207\t  };\n208\t\n209\t  const readString = (maxLen: number) => {\n210\t    const len = readU8();\n211\t    if (len > maxLen) {\n212\t      throw new BeaconValidationError(\n213\t        'E-META-BOUNDS',\n214\t        `String length ${len} exceeds maximum ${maxLen}`,\n215\t        {length: len, max: maxLen}\n216\t      );\n217\t    }\n218\t    const strBytes = readBytes(len);\n219\t    // Decode as UTF-8\n220\t    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true});\n221\t    try {\n222\t      return decoder.decode(strBytes);\n223\t    } catch (e) {\n224\t      throw new BeaconValidationError(\n225\t        'E-META-BOUNDS',\n226\t        'Invalid UTF-8 in string field',\n227\t        {}\n228\t      );\n229\t    }\n230\t  };\n231\t\n232\t  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 1: Read all fields (no validation yet)\n233\t\n234\t  const streamId = readU32();\n235\t  const wireVersion = readU8();\n236\t  // originalSize: 6 bytes, 48-bit (original uncompressed file size)\n237\t  const originalSize = ((readU8() << 40) & 0xff0000000000) |\n238\t                       ((readU32() << 8) & 0xffffffffff00) |\n239\t                       (readU8() & 0xff);\n240\t  // payloadLen: 6 bytes, 48-bit (actual payload length after compression)\n241\t  const payloadLen = ((readU8() << 40) & 0xff0000000000) |\n242\t                     ((readU32() << 8) & 0xffffffffff00) |\n243\t                     (readU8() & 0xff);\n244\t\n245\t  const blockSize = readU24();\n246\t  const blockCount = readU24();\n247\t  const fragmentLen = readU16();\n248\t  const degreeCap = readU8();\n249\t  const flags = readU8();\n250\t  const blockHashLen = readU8();\n251\t  const wholeFileHash = readBytes(32); // Fixed 32-byte whole file hash\n252\t  const manifestHash = readBytes(4); // Fixed 4-byte manifest hash (CRC-32)\n253\t\n254\t  // Variable fields\n255\t  const filename = readString(BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES);\n256\t  const mimeType = readString(BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES);\n257\t\n258\t  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 2: CRC-32 validation (MUST pass before any beacon values are trusted)\n259\t\n260\t  const CRC_SIZE = 4;\n261\t  const beaconBodySize = offset;\n262\t  const expectedSize = beaconBodySize + CRC_SIZE;\n263\t\n264\t  if (bytes.length !== expectedSize) {\n265\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n266\t      'E-CRC-LENGTH',\n267\t      `Beacon size ${bytes.length} != expected ${expectedSize} (body+CRC)`,\n268\t      {actual: bytes.length, expected: expectedSize}\n269\t    );\n270\t  }\n271\t\n272\t  // Read the CRC-32 from the last 4 bytes\n273\t  const storedCrc = ((bytes[offset]! << 24) |\n274\t                    (bytes[offset + 1]! << 16) |\n275\t                    (bytes[offset + 2]! << 8) |\n276\t                    bytes[offset + 3]!) >>> 0; // Force unsigned\n277\t\n278\t  // Calculate CRC-32 over the beacon body (everything except the last 4 bytes)\n279\t  const calculatedCrc = crc32(bytes.subarray(0, beaconBodySize));\n280\t\n281\t  if (storedCrc !== calculatedCrc) {\n282\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n283\t      'E-CRC-MISMATCH',\n284\t      `Beacon CRC-32 mismatch: stored=${storedCrc} calculated=${calculatedCrc}`,\n285\t      {stored: storedCrc, calculated: calculatedCrc}\n286\t    );\n287\t  }\n288\t\n289\t  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 3: T1/META bounds checks (now safe because CRC validated)\n290\t\n291\t  // T1: originalSize bounds check\n292\t  if (originalSize > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE) {\n293\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n294\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n295\t      `Declared original size (${originalSize}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE})`,\n296\t      {originalSize, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE}\n297\t    );\n298\t  }\n299\t\n300\t  // T1: originalSize must fit in available quota\n301\t  if (originalSize > availableQuota) {\n302\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n303\t      'E-QUOTA-PREFLIGHT',\n304\t      `Original size (${originalSize}) exceeds available quota (${availableQuota})`,\n305\t      {originalSize, availableQuota}\n306\t    );\n307\t  }\n308\t\n309\t  // T1: payloadLen bounds check (same limit as originalSize)\n310\t  if (payloadLen > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE) {\n311\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n312\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n313\t      `Declared payload length (${payloadLen}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE})`,\n314\t      {payloadLen, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE}\n315\t    );\n316\t  }\n317\t\n318\t  // T1: payloadLen must fit in available quota\n319\t  if (payloadLen > availableQuota) {\n320\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n321\t      'E-QUOTA-PREFLIGHT',\n322\t      `Payload length (${payloadLen}) exceeds available quota (${availableQuota})`,\n323\t      {payloadLen, availableQuota}\n324\t    );\n325\t  }\n326\t\n327\t  // Sanity: payloadLen should be ≤ originalSize (compression can only reduce)\n328\t  if (payloadLen > originalSize) {\n329\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n330\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n331\t      `Payload length (${payloadLen}) cannot exceed original size (${originalSize})`,\n332\t      {payloadLen, originalSize}\n333\t    );\n334\t  }\n335\t\n336\t  // T1: blockCount bounds check\n337\t  if (blockCount > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT) {\n338\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n339\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n340\t      `Block count (${blockCount}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT})`,\n341\t      {blockCount, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT}\n342\t    );\n343\t  }\n344\t\n345\t  // T1: K_manifest bounds check (bf-5fs)\n346\t  // blockCount_manifest is the number of blocks needed to store the block hash manifest.\n347\t  // Unbounded blockCount_manifest is a DoS vector: with MAX_BLOCK_COUNT (16.7M) and blockHashLen=4,\n348\t  // the manifest would require 340 blocks (~66 MB manifest data, ~24 KB matrix per block).\n349\t  const blockCountManifest = calculateKManifest(blockCount, blockHashLen);\n350\t  if (blockCountManifest > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS) {\n351\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n352\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n353\t      `Manifest block count (${blockCountManifest}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS}). ` +\n354\t      `This would require ${blockCountManifest} blocks (${(blockCountManifest * 196608 / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB) ` +\n355\t      `to store the manifest for ${blockCount} data blocks.`,\n356\t      {\n357\t        blockCount,\n358\t        blockHashLen,\n359\t        blockCountManifest,\n360\t        max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS\n361\t      }\n362\t    );\n363\t  }\n364\t\n365\t  // Sanity: blockSize should be reasonable\n366\t  if (blockSize < 1 || blockSize > 10_485_760) { // Max 10 MB per block\n367\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n368\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n369\t      `Invalid block size: ${blockSize}`,\n370\t      {blockSize}\n371\t    );\n372\t  }\n373\t\n374\t  // T1: Wire version compatibility check\n375\t  if (wireVersion !== WIRE_VERSION) {\n376\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n377\t      'E-VERSION',\n378\t      `Wire version mismatch: sender is ${wireVersion}, receiver is ${WIRE_VERSION}`,\n379\t      {senderVersion: wireVersion, receiverVersion: WIRE_VERSION}\n380\t    );\n381\t  }\n382\t\n383\t  // T1: L must match wire constant for this version\n384\t  if (fragmentLen !== L) {\n385\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n386\t      'E-VERSION',\n387\t      `Fragment length L mismatch: sender declared ${fragmentLen}, wire constant is ${L}`,\n388\t      {senderL: fragmentLen, wireConstantL: L, wireVersion}\n389\t    );\n390\t  }\n391\t\n392\t  // T1: Secondary sanity check (should never fire if above checks pass)\n393\t  if (fragmentLen < 1 || fragmentLen > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L) {\n394\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n395\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n396\t      `Fragment length L (${fragmentLen}) outside valid range [1, ${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L}]`,\n397\t      {L: fragmentLen, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L}\n398\t    );\n399\t  }\n400\t\n401\t  // Sanity: degreeCap should be within reasonable bounds (D25 says 64)\n402\t  if (degreeCap < 1 || degreeCap > 256) {\n403\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n404\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n405\t      `Invalid degree cap: ${degreeCap}`,\n406\t      {degreeCap}\n407\t    );\n408\t  }\n409\t\n410\t  // Sanity: blockHashLen should be reasonable (SHA-256 truncated to 4 bytes per plan)\n411\t  if (blockHashLen < 1 || blockHashLen > 64) {\n412\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n413\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n414\t      `Invalid block hash length: ${blockHashLen}`,\n415\t      {blockHashLen}\n416\t    );\n417\t  }\n418\t\n419\t  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 4: D26/T1: K validation\n420\t\n421\t  // Derive K from blockSize and L\n422\t  const kValidation = validateBeaconK(blockSize, fragmentLen, localKMax, deviceContext);\n423\t\n424\t  if (!kValidation.acceptable) {\n425\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n426\t      kValidation.error!.code,\n427\t      kValidation.error!.message,\n428\t      kValidation.error!.details\n429\t    );\n430\t  }\n431\t\n432\t  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 5: Sanity checks\n433\t\n434\t  // Sanity: blockCount × blockSize should approximately equal payloadLen\n435\t  // (last block may be short, so allow ±1 block tolerance)\n436\t  const estimatedSize = (blockCount - 1) * blockSize;\n437\t  if (payloadLen < estimatedSize - blockSize || payloadLen > blockCount * blockSize + blockSize) {\n438\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n439\t      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n440\t      `Inconsistent beacon: payloadLen=${payloadLen}, blockCount=${blockCount}, blockSize=${blockSize}`,\n441\t      {payloadLen, blockCount, blockSize}\n442\t    );\n443\t  }\n444\t\n445\t  return {\n446\t    streamId,\n447\t    wireVersion,\n448\t    originalSize,\n449\t    payloadLen,\n450\t    blockSize,\n451\t    blockCount,\n452\t    fragmentLen,\n453\t    degreeCap,\n454\t    flags,\n455\t    blockHashLen,\n456\t    wholeFileHash,\n457\t    manifestHash,\n458\t    filename,\n459\t    mimeType,\n460\t  };\n461\t}\n462\t\n463\t/**\n464\t * Sanitize a filename for export (T2).\n465\t *\n466\t * **Truncation rules (T2):**\n467\t * 1. Strip path separators, control bytes, and leading dots (security)\n468\t * 2. Truncate to MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS (32 UTF-8 codepoints max)\n469\t * 3. If truncated, preserve filename extension when possible\n470\t * 4. Validate final UTF-8 encoding fits in MAX_FILENAME_BYTES (128 bytes)\n471\t * 5. Fall back to \"received-file\" if empty after sanitization\n472\t *\n473\t * **Why both codepoint and byte limits:**\n474\t * - UTF-8 uses 1-4 bytes per codepoint\n475\t * - MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS guarantees we never exceed MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n476\t * - MAX_FILENAME_BYTES ensures the beacon fits in R1's 256-byte payload\n477\t *\n478\t * @param filename - Attacker-supplied filename from beacon\n479\t * @returns Sanitized filename safe for filesystem export and beacon encoding\n480\t */\n481\texport function sanitizeFilename(filename: string): string {\n482\t  // Step 1: Remove path separators (prevents directory traversal)\n483\t  let sanitized = filename.replace(/[\\/\\\\]/g, '_');\n484\t\n485\t  // Step 2: Remove control bytes (0x00-0x1F, 0x7F) — invalid on most filesystems\n486\t  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/[\\x00-\\x1F\\x7F]/g, '');\n487\t\n488\t  // Step 3: Strip leading dots (avoid hidden files on Unix, prevent dotfiles attack)\n489\t  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/^\\.+/g, '');\n490\t\n491\t  // Step 4: Truncate to MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS (32 codepoints)\n492\t  if (sanitized.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS) {\n493\t    // Try to preserve extension: find last dot and keep extension\n494\t    const lastDot = sanitized.lastIndexOf('.');\n495\t\n496\t    if (lastDot > 0 && lastDot < BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS - 5) {\n497\t      // Keep extension: truncate base, preserve \".ext\"\n498\t      const ext = sanitized.substring(lastDot);\n499\t      const baseAllowed = BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS - ext.length;\n500\t      sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, baseAllowed) + ext;\n501\t    } else {\n502\t      // No extension or extension too long: simple truncate\n503\t      sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS);\n504\t    }\n505\t  }\n506\t\n507\t  // Step 5: Validate UTF-8 byte length fits in MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n508\t  const utf8Bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(sanitized);\n509\t  if (utf8Bytes.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES) {\n510\t    // Byte overflow: truncate conservatively to UTF-8 safe boundary\n511\t    // Conservative: each codepoint can be up to 4 bytes, so truncate to codepoints that fit\n512\t    let byteLen = 0;\n513\t    let cpIndex = 0;\n514\t    for (let i = 0; i < sanitized.length; i++) {\n515\t      const cpBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(sanitized[i]).length;\n516\t      if (byteLen + cpBytes > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES) break;\n517\t      byteLen += cpBytes;\n518\t      cpIndex = i + 1;\n519\t    }\n520\t    sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, cpIndex);\n521\t  }\n522\t\n523\t  // Step 6: Fallback if empty after sanitization\n524\t  if (!sanitized) {\n525\t    sanitized = 'received-file';\n526\t  }\n527\t\n528\t  return sanitized;\n529\t}\n530\t\n531\t/**\n532\t * Check if resume should be disabled based on beacon flags.\n533\t *\n534\t * When compression is enabled, the sender MUST set BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled\n535\t * because non-deterministic compression makes block boundaries unstable across\n536\t * sender restarts. This prevents silent corruption of the receiver's bitmap.\n537\t *\n538\t * **How this works:**\n539\t * 1. Sender enables compression → sets both Compressed and ResumeDisabled flags\n540\t * 2. Receiver receives beacon → checks isResumeDisabled() → returns true\n541\t * 3. Receiver suppresses resume UI and does NOT persist bitmap/metadata\n542\t * 4. If interrupted, user must restart transfer from beginning (safe, no corruption)\n543\t *\n544\t * **Why this is necessary:**\n545\t * CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee. After a sender restart and\n546\t * E11 staging reaping, re-compression may produce different bytes → different\n547\t * block boundaries → different hashes → the receiver's persisted bitmap would\n548\t * become silently invalid.\n549\t *\n550\t * Solution implemented: Option B from bf-3k90 evaluation\n551\t * - Privacy (T4) preserved: no staging persistence\n552\t * - Correctness preserved: explicitly disabling unsafe resume\n553\t * - Low complexity: ~50-100 lines vs. 300-1200 for alternatives\n554\t *\n555\t * Reference: docs/notes/bf-3k90-compression-resume-solution-evaluation.md (Option B)\n556\t *            docs/notes/bf-2vke-compression-resume-t4-reap-interaction.md\n557\t *            docs/notes/bf-17s0-resume-compression-conflict.md\n558\t *\n559\t * @param flags - Beacon flags byte from received beacon\n560\t * @returns true if resume is disabled (compression enabled), false otherwise\n561\t */\n562\texport function isResumeDisabled(flags: number): boolean {\n563\t  return (flags & BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled) !== 0;\n564\t}\n565\t\n566\t/**\n567\t * Validate compression and resume flag compatibility (bf-2a1rz).\n568\t *\n569\t * **Pattern Reference:** bf-3zoz4 - Pure validation function that throws errors\n570\t * before any state changes occur. This prevents unsafe combinations of flags.\n571\t *\n572\t * **Conflict Condition:** Compression enabled BUT resume not disabled.\n573\t * When BeaconFlags.Compressed is set, BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled MUST also be set.\n574\t *\n575\t * **Why this is unsafe:**\n576\t * CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee across browser restarts.\n577\t * After a sender restart and E11 staging reaping, re-compression may produce\n578\t * different bytes → different block boundaries → different hashes → the\n579\t * receiver's persisted bitmap becomes silently invalid.\n580\t *\n581\t * **Reference:**\n582\t * - validateBeaconMeta(meta: BeaconMeta): void {\n627\t  // Wire version validation\n628\t  if (meta.wireVersion !== WIRE_VERSION) {\n629\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n630\t      'E-VERSION',\n631\t      `Cannot encode beacon for wire version ${meta.wireVersion}, this implementation is ${WIRE_VERSION}`,\n632\t      {requestedVersion: meta.wireVersion, supportedVersion: WIRE_VERSION}\n633\t    );\n634\t  }\n635\t\n636\t  // Fragment length validation\n637\t  if (meta.fragmentLen !== L) {\n638\t    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n639\t      'E-VERSION',\n640\t `Cannot encode beacon with fragmentLen ${meta.fragmentLen}, wire constant is ${L}`,\n689\t      {requestedL: meta.fragmentLen, wireConstantL: L, wireVersion: meta.wireVersion}\n690\t    );\n691\t  }\n692\t\n693\t  // bf-4bi6: Validate compression/resume conflict before any state changes\n694\t  // This check prevents the unsafe combination where compression is enabled\n695\t  // but resume is * Beacon frame parsing and validation.\n *\n * Implements D17/D21 beacon format with D26/T1 bounds checking including\n * K validation against the local device's benchmarked K_max.\n *\n * Reference: plan.md §7.2, D26, T1, §16.4\n */\n\nimport {validateBeaconK} from '../../platform/ge-benchmark.js';\nimport {crc32} from './crc.js';\nimport {L, WIRE_VERSION} from '../params.js';\n\n/**\n * Beacon flags (1 byte).\n *\n * SENDER CONSTRAINT: When compression is enabled, you MUST set BOTH Compressed\n * AND ResumeDisabled flags. This is required because CompressionStream offers\n * no determinism guarantee across browser restarts, making resume unsafe.\n *\n * Usage (when constructing beacon for transmission):\n * ```typescript\n * let flags = BeaconFlags.None;\n * if (compressionEnabled) {\n *   flags = BeaconFlags.Compressed | BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled;\n * }\n * const meta: BeaconMeta = { ..., flags };\n * const beaconBytes = encodeBeacon(meta);\n * ```\n *\n * See: encodeBeacon() documentation for full rationale and references.\n */\nexport enum BeaconFlags {\n  None = 0,\n  /**\n   * Compression enabled (D8).\n   * When set, ResumeDisabled MUST also be set (see encodeBeacon docs).\n   */\n  Compressed = 1 << 0,\n  /**\n   * Resume is disabled.\n   * MUST be set when compression is enabled (CompressionStream is non-deterministic).\n   * Receiver checks this via isResumeDisabled() to suppress resume UI and prevent\n   * persisting bitmap/metadata that would become silently invalid after sender restart.\n   */\n  ResumeDisabled = 1 << 1,\n  /** Hash algorithm bitmask */\n  HashMask = 0b11110000,\n}\n\n/**\n * Parsed beacon metadata.\n */\nexport interface BeaconMeta {\n  streamId: number;\n  wireVersion: number;\n  originalSize: number; // Original uncompressed file size\n  payloadLen: number; // Actual payload length (after compression if enabled)\n  blockSize: number;\n  blockCount: number;\n  fragmentLen: number; // L\n  degreeCap: number;\n  flags: number;\n  blockHashLen: number;\n  wholeFileHash: Uint8Array; // 32 bytes\n  manifestHash: Uint8Array; // 4 bytes - CRC-32 of manifest (roots beacon->manifest->blocks chain)\n  filename: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Validation error with code and details.\n */\nexport class BeaconValidationError extends Error {\n  constructor(\n    public code: string,\n    message: string,\n    public details: Record<string, unknown>\n  ) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = 'BeaconValidationError';\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Maximum safe values for beacon fields (T1).\n *\n * **Beacon size bound calculation (R1's 256-byte payload):**\n * - Fixed fields: 64 bytes (streamId, wireVersion, originalSize, payloadLen, blockSize,\n *                  blockCount, fragmentLen, degreeCap, flags, blockHashLen,\n *                  wholeFileHash, manifestHash)\n * - CRC-32: 4 bytes\n * - Length prefixes: 2 bytes (1 byte each for filename and mimeType)\n * - Available for filename + mimeType: 256 - 64 - 4 - 2 = 186 bytes\n *\n * This yields a conservative allocation of 128 bytes for filename and 58 bytes for\n * mimeType, ensuring the beacon never overflows R1's capacity while accommodating\n * common filenames and MIME types.\n */\nexport const BEACON_LIMITS = {\n  MAX_L: 4096,\n  MAX_BLOCK_COUNT: 16_700_000, // 16.7M blocks × 192 KB = 3.0 TB\n  MAX_FILE_SIZE: 281_474_976_710_656, // 281 TB, 6-byte field max\n  /** Maximum number of blocks that the manifest itself can consume (T1). */\n  /** K_manifest = ceil(blockCount × blockHashLen / BLOCK). Limits DoS from unbounded manifest growth. */\n  MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS: 1000, // 1000 manifest blocks ≈ 195 MB of manifest data\n  /** Maximum UTF-8 encoded filename length in bytes (T2). Chosen to fit within R1's 256-byte payload. */\n  MAX_FILENAME_BYTES: 128,\n  /** Maximum UTF-8 encoded MIME type length in bytes (T2). Chosen to fit within R1's 256-byte payload. */\n  MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES: 58,\n  /** Maximum UTF-8 codepoint count for filename (T2). A UTF-8 string can use up to 4 bytes per codepoint. */\n  MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS: 32, // 32 × 4 = 128, guarantees never exceeds MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n  /** Maximum UTF-8 codepoint count for MIME type (T2). */\n  MAX_MIMETYPE_CODEPOINTS: 14, // 14 × 4 = 56, fits within MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * Calculate K_manifest from blockCount and blockHashLen.\n *\n * K_manifest is the number of blocks needed to store the block hash manifest.\n * The manifest contains blockCount × blockHashLen bytes (all block hashes),\n * divided into blocks of size BLOCK (196608 bytes = K × L).\n *\n * Formula: K_manifest = ceil(blockCount × blockHashLen / BLOCK)\n *\n * @param blockCount - Number of data blocks (from beacon)\n * @param blockHashLen - Length of each block hash in bytes (from beacon)\n * @returns Number of blocks needed to store the manifest\n */\nfunction calculateKManifest(blockCount: number, blockHashLen: number): number {\n  const BLOCK = 196608; // K × L where K=768, L=256 (from core/params.ts)\n  const manifestBytes = blockCount * blockHashLen;\n  return Math.ceil(manifestBytes / BLOCK);\n}\n\n/**\n * Device context for K validation logging.\n */\nexport interface DeviceContext {\n  /** Device signature from GE benchmark cache */\n  deviceSignature: string;\n  /** User agent string */\n  userAgent: string;\n  /** Platform identifier */\n  platform: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Parse a beacon from bytes.\n *\n * Beacon format: [body fields...][crc32(4)]\n * The CRC-32 covers all beacon body fields and MUST be validated before\n * any metadata (including streamId) is trusted.\n *\n * **Validation order is critical:**\n * 1. Read all fields (no validation yet)\n * 2. Validate CRC-32 (if this fails, ALL other values are suspect)\n * 3. Then do bounds/K/quota checks (safe now because CRC validated)\n *\n * @param bytes - Beacon payload bytes (after QR decoding and header stripping)\n * @param localKMax - This device's benchmarked maximum K (from GE benchmark)\n * @param availableQuota - Available storage quota in bytes (for T1 originalSize check)\n * @param deviceContext - Optional device context for K validation logging\n * @throws {BeaconValidationError} If any field fails validation or CRC-32 mismatch\n */\nexport function parseBeacon(\n  bytes: Uint8Array,\n  localKMax: number,\n  availableQuota: number,\n  deviceContext?: DeviceContext\n): BeaconMeta {\n  let offset = 0;\n\n  const readU32 = (): number => {\n    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 24) |\n                 (bytes[offset + 1]! << 16) |\n                 (bytes[offset + 2]! << 8) |\n                 bytes[offset + 3]!;\n    offset += 4;\n    return value >>> 0; // Force unsigned\n  };\n\n  const readU24 = (): number => {\n    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 16) |\n                 (bytes[offset + 1]! << 8) |\n                 bytes[offset + 2]!;\n    offset += 3;\n    return value >>> 0;\n  };\n\n  const readU16 = (): number => {\n    const value = (bytes[offset]! << 8) | bytes[offset + 1]!;\n    offset += 2;\n    return value >>> 0;\n  };\n\n  const readU8 = (): number => {\n    const value = bytes[offset]!;\n    offset += 1;\n    return value;\n  };\n\n  const readBytes = (n: number): Uint8Array => {\n    const value = bytes.subarray(offset, offset + n);\n    offset += n;\n    return value;\n  };\n\n  const readString = (maxLen: number) => {\n    const len = readU8();\n    if (len > maxLen) {\n      throw new BeaconValidationError(\n        'E-META-BOUNDS',\n        `String length ${len} exceeds maximum ${maxLen}`,\n        {length: len, max: maxLen}\n      );\n    }\n    const strBytes = readBytes(len);\n    // Decode as UTF-8\n    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true});\n    try {\n      return decoder.decode(strBytes);\n    } catch (e) {\n      throw new BeaconValidationError(\n        'E-META-BOUNDS',\n        'Invalid UTF-8 in string field',\n        {}\n      );\n    }\n  };\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 1: Read all fields (no validation yet)\n\n  const streamId = readU32();\n  const wireVersion = readU8();\n  // originalSize: 6 bytes, 48-bit (original uncompressed file size)\n  const originalSize = ((readU8() << 40) & 0xff0000000000) |\n                       ((readU32() << 8) & 0xffffffffff00) |\n                       (readU8() & 0xff);\n  // payloadLen: 6 bytes, 48-bit (actual payload length after compression)\n  const payloadLen = ((readU8() << 40) & 0xff0000000000) |\n                     ((readU32() << 8) & 0xffffffffff00) |\n                     (readU8() & 0xff);\n\n  const blockSize = readU24();\n  const blockCount = readU24();\n  const fragmentLen = readU16();\n  const degreeCap = readU8();\n  const flags = readU8();\n  const blockHashLen = readU8();\n  const wholeFileHash = readBytes(32); // Fixed 32-byte whole file hash\n  const manifestHash = readBytes(4); // Fixed 4-byte manifest hash (CRC-32)\n\n  // Variable fields\n  const filename = readString(BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES);\n  const mimeType = readString(BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES);\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 2: CRC-32 validation (MUST pass before any beacon values are trusted)\n\n  const CRC_SIZE = 4;\n  const beaconBodySize = offset;\n  const expectedSize = beaconBodySize + CRC_SIZE;\n\n  if (bytes.length !== expectedSize) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-CRC-LENGTH',\n      `Beacon size ${bytes.length} != expected ${expectedSize} (body+CRC)`,\n      {actual: bytes.length, expected: expectedSize}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Read the CRC-32 from the last 4 bytes\n  const storedCrc = ((bytes[offset]! << 24) |\n                    (bytes[offset + 1]! << 16) |\n                    (bytes[offset + 2]! << 8) |\n                    bytes[offset + 3]!) >>> 0; // Force unsigned\n\n  // Calculate CRC-32 over the beacon body (everything except the last 4 bytes)\n  const calculatedCrc = crc32(bytes.subarray(0, beaconBodySize));\n\n  if (storedCrc !== calculatedCrc) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-CRC-MISMATCH',\n      `Beacon CRC-32 mismatch: stored=${storedCrc} calculated=${calculatedCrc}`,\n      {stored: storedCrc, calculated: calculatedCrc}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 3: T1/META bounds checks (now safe because CRC validated)\n\n  // T1: originalSize bounds check\n  if (originalSize > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Declared original size (${originalSize}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE})`,\n      {originalSize, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: originalSize must fit in available quota\n  if (originalSize > availableQuota) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-QUOTA-PREFLIGHT',\n      `Original size (${originalSize}) exceeds available quota (${availableQuota})`,\n      {originalSize, availableQuota}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: payloadLen bounds check (same limit as originalSize)\n  if (payloadLen > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Declared payload length (${payloadLen}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE})`,\n      {payloadLen, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILE_SIZE}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: payloadLen must fit in available quota\n  if (payloadLen > availableQuota) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-QUOTA-PREFLIGHT',\n      `Payload length (${payloadLen}) exceeds available quota (${availableQuota})`,\n      {payloadLen, availableQuota}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Sanity: payloadLen should be ≤ originalSize (compression can only reduce)\n  if (payloadLen > originalSize) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Payload length (${payloadLen}) cannot exceed original size (${originalSize})`,\n      {payloadLen, originalSize}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: blockCount bounds check\n  if (blockCount > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Block count (${blockCount}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT})`,\n      {blockCount, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_BLOCK_COUNT}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: K_manifest bounds check (bf-5fs)\n  // blockCount_manifest is the number of blocks needed to store the block hash manifest.\n  // Unbounded blockCount_manifest is a DoS vector: with MAX_BLOCK_COUNT (16.7M) and blockHashLen=4,\n  // the manifest would require 340 blocks (~66 MB manifest data, ~24 KB matrix per block).\n  const blockCountManifest = calculateKManifest(blockCount, blockHashLen);\n  if (blockCountManifest > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Manifest block count (${blockCountManifest}) exceeds maximum (${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS}). ` +\n      `This would require ${blockCountManifest} blocks (${(blockCountManifest * 196608 / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB) ` +\n      `to store the manifest for ${blockCount} data blocks.`,\n      {\n        blockCount,\n        blockHashLen,\n        blockCountManifest,\n        max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_K_MANIFEST_BLOCKS\n      }\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Sanity: blockSize should be reasonable\n  if (blockSize < 1 || blockSize > 10_485_760) { // Max 10 MB per block\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Invalid block size: ${blockSize}`,\n      {blockSize}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: Wire version compatibility check\n  if (wireVersion !== WIRE_VERSION) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Wire version mismatch: sender is ${wireVersion}, receiver is ${WIRE_VERSION}`,\n      {senderVersion: wireVersion, receiverVersion: WIRE_VERSION}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: L must match wire constant for this version\n  if (fragmentLen !== L) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Fragment length L mismatch: sender declared ${fragmentLen}, wire constant is ${L}`,\n      {senderL: fragmentLen, wireConstantL: L, wireVersion}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // T1: Secondary sanity check (should never fire if above checks pass)\n  if (fragmentLen < 1 || fragmentLen > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Fragment length L (${fragmentLen}) outside valid range [1, ${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L}]`,\n      {L: fragmentLen, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_L}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Sanity: degreeCap should be within reasonable bounds (D25 says 64)\n  if (degreeCap < 1 || degreeCap > 256) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Invalid degree cap: ${degreeCap}`,\n      {degreeCap}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Sanity: blockHashLen should be reasonable (SHA-256 truncated to 4 bytes per plan)\n  if (blockHashLen < 1 || blockHashLen > 64) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Invalid block hash length: ${blockHashLen}`,\n      {blockHashLen}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 4: D26/T1: K validation\n\n  // Derive K from blockSize and L\n  const kValidation = validateBeaconK(blockSize, fragmentLen, localKMax, deviceContext);\n\n  if (!kValidation.acceptable) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      kValidation.error!.code,\n      kValidation.error!.message,\n      kValidation.error!.details\n    );\n  }\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ STEP 5: Sanity checks\n\n  // Sanity: blockCount × blockSize should approximately equal payloadLen\n  // (last block may be short, so allow ±1 block tolerance)\n  const estimatedSize = (blockCount - 1) * blockSize;\n  if (payloadLen < estimatedSize - blockSize || payloadLen > blockCount * blockSize + blockSize) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Inconsistent beacon: payloadLen=${payloadLen}, blockCount=${blockCount}, blockSize=${blockSize}`,\n      {payloadLen, blockCount, blockSize}\n    );\n  }\n\n  return {\n    streamId,\n    wireVersion,\n    originalSize,\n    payloadLen,\n    blockSize,\n    blockCount,\n    fragmentLen,\n    degreeCap,\n    flags,\n    blockHashLen,\n    wholeFileHash,\n    manifestHash,\n    filename,\n    mimeType,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Sanitize a filename for export (T2).\n *\n * **Truncation rules (T2):**\n * 1. Strip path separators, control bytes, and leading dots (security)\n * 2. Truncate to MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS (32 UTF-8 codepoints max)\n * 3. If truncated, preserve filename extension when possible\n * 4. Validate final UTF-8 encoding fits in MAX_FILENAME_BYTES (128 bytes)\n * 5. Fall back to \"received-file\" if empty after sanitization\n *\n * **Why both codepoint and byte limits:**\n * - UTF-8 uses 1-4 bytes per codepoint\n * - MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS guarantees we never exceed MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n * - MAX_FILENAME_BYTES ensures the beacon fits in R1's 256-byte payload\n *\n * @param filename - Attacker-supplied filename from beacon\n * @returns Sanitized filename safe for filesystem export and beacon encoding\n */\nexport function sanitizeFilename(filename: string): string {\n  // Step 1: Remove path separators (prevents directory traversal)\n  let sanitized = filename.replace(/[\\/\\\\]/g, '_');\n\n  // Step 2: Remove control bytes (0x00-0x1F, 0x7F) — invalid on most filesystems\n  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/[\\x00-\\x1F\\x7F]/g, '');\n\n  // Step 3: Strip leading dots (avoid hidden files on Unix, prevent dotfiles attack)\n  sanitized = sanitized.replace(/^\\.+/g, '');\n\n  // Step 4: Truncate to MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS (32 codepoints)\n  if (sanitized.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS) {\n    // Try to preserve extension: find last dot and keep extension\n    const lastDot = sanitized.lastIndexOf('.');\n\n    if (lastDot > 0 && lastDot < BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS - 5) {\n      // Keep extension: truncate base, preserve \".ext\"\n      const ext = sanitized.substring(lastDot);\n      const baseAllowed = BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS - ext.length;\n      sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, baseAllowed) + ext;\n    } else {\n      // No extension or extension too long: simple truncate\n      sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_CODEPOINTS);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Step 5: Validate UTF-8 byte length fits in MAX_FILENAME_BYTES\n  const utf8Bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(sanitized);\n  if (utf8Bytes.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES) {\n    // Byte overflow: truncate conservatively to UTF-8 safe boundary\n    // Conservative: each codepoint can be up to 4 bytes, so truncate to codepoints that fit\n    let byteLen = 0;\n    let cpIndex = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < sanitized.length; i++) {\n      const cpBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(sanitized[i]).length;\n      if (byteLen + cpBytes > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES) break;\n      byteLen += cpBytes;\n      cpIndex = i + 1;\n    }\n    sanitized = sanitized.substring(0, cpIndex);\n  }\n\n  // Step 6: Fallback if empty after sanitization\n  if (!sanitized) {\n    sanitized = 'received-file';\n  }\n\n  return sanitized;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if resume should be disabled based on beacon flags.\n *\n * When compression is enabled, the sender MUST set BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled\n * because non-deterministic compression makes block boundaries unstable across\n * sender restarts. This prevents silent corruption of the receiver's bitmap.\n *\n * **How this works:**\n * 1. Sender enables compression → sets both Compressed and ResumeDisabled flags\n * 2. Receiver receives beacon → checks isResumeDisabled() → returns true\n * 3. Receiver suppresses resume UI and does NOT persist bitmap/metadata\n * 4. If interrupted, user must restart transfer from beginning (safe, no corruption)\n *\n * **Why this is necessary:**\n * CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee. After a sender restart and\n * E11 staging reaping, re-compression may produce different bytes → different\n * block boundaries → different hashes → the receiver's persisted bitmap would\n * become silently invalid.\n *\n * Solution implemented: Option B from bf-3k90 evaluation\n * - Privacy (T4) preserved: no staging persistence\n * - Correctness preserved: explicitly disabling unsafe resume\n * - Low complexity: ~50-100 lines vs. 300-1200 for alternatives\n *\n * Reference: docs/notes/bf-3k90-compression-resume-solution-evaluation.md (Option B)\n *            docs/notes/bf-2vke-compression-resume-t4-reap-interaction.md\n *            docs/notes/bf-17s0-resume-compression-conflict.md\n *\n * @param flags - Beacon flags byte from received beacon\n * @returns true if resume is disabled (compression enabled), false otherwise\n */\nexport function isResumeDisabled(flags: number): boolean {\n  return (flags & BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled) !== 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate compression and resume flag compatibility (bf-2a1rz).\n *\n * **Pattern Reference:** bf-3zoz4 - Pure validation function that throws errors\n * before any state changes occur. This prevents unsafe combinations of flags.\n *\n * **Conflict Condition:** Compression enabled BUT resume not disabled.\n * When BeaconFlags.Compressed is set, BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled MUST also be set.\n *\n * **Why this is unsafe:**\n * CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee across browser restarts.\n * After a sender restart and E11 staging reaping, re-compression may produce\n * different bytes → different block boundaries → different hashes → the\n * receiver's persisted bitmap becomes silently invalid.\n *\n * **Reference:**\n * - Pattern: docs/notes/bf-3zoz4-conflict-check-pattern.md\n * - Analysis: docs/notes/bf-17s0-resume-compression-conflict.md\n * - Implementation: bf-4bi6\n *\n * @param flags - Beacon flags bitmask to validate\n * @throws {BeaconValidationError} If compression is enabled but resume is not disabled\n */\nexport function validateCompressionResumeConflict(flags: number): void {\n  const compressionEnabled = (flags & BeaconFlags.Compressed) !== 0;\n  const resumeDisabled = (flags & BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled) !== 0;\n\n  if (compressionEnabled && !resumeDisabled) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-COMPRESSION-RESUME-CONFLICT',\n      `E-COMPRESSION-RESUME-CONFLICT: Compression cannot be enabled without disabling resume. ` +\n      `When BeaconFlags.Compressed is set, BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled must also be set. ` +\n      `This incompatibility is required because CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee across ` +\n      `browser restarts, making resume unsafe (see bf-17s0, bf-2w1a).`,\n      {\n        flags,\n        compressionEnabled,\n        resumeDisabled,\n      }\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate beacon metadata for encoding (bf-2a1rz).\n *\n * **Path 1:** Entry point validation for beacon construction.\n * Performs all validation checks before encoding to prevent invalid state.\n *\n * **Validations performed:**\n * 1. Wire version compatibility\n * 2. Fragment length (L) consistency\n * 3. Compression/resume flag compatibility (via validateCompressionResumeConflict)\n *\n * **Pattern:** Follows bf-3zoz4 pattern - pure validation that throws before\n * any state changes, with structured error details.\n *\n * @param meta - Beacon metadata to validate\n * @throws {BeaconValidationError} If any validation fails\n */\nexport function validateBeaconMeta(meta: BeaconMeta): void {\n  // Wire version validation\n  if (meta.wireVersion !== WIRE_VERSION) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Cannot encode beacon for wire version ${meta.wireVersion}, this implementation is ${WIRE_VERSION}`,\n      {requestedVersion: meta.wireVersion, supportedVersion: WIRE_VERSION}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Fragment length validation\n  if (meta.fragmentLen !== L) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Cannot encode beacon with fragmentLen ${meta.fragmentLen}, wire constant is ${L}`,\n      {requestedL: meta.fragmentLen, wireConstantL: L, wireVersion: meta.wireVersion}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Compression/resume conflict validation (bf-2a1rz)\n  validateCompressionResumeConflict(meta.flags);\n}\n\n/**\n * Encode a beacon from metadata.\n *\n * Serializes beacon metadata into bytes and appends a CRC-32 checksum\n * over the beacon body for integrity validation.\n *\n * **SENDER CONSTRAINT:** When constructing a BeaconMeta object to pass to this function:\n * - If compression is enabled, you MUST set both flags:\n *   `flags = BeaconFlags.Compressed | BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled`\n * - This is required because CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee\n *   across browser restarts, making resume unsafe (see below)\n *\n * **Why compression disables resume:**\n * Non-deterministic compression means that after a sender restart and E11 staging\n * reaping, re-compression may produce different bytes → different block boundaries\n * → different hashes → the receiver's persisted bitmap becomes silently invalid.\n *\n * Solution: The sender signals \"no resume available\" via the beacon flag, and the\n * receiver suppresses resume UI and does not persist the bitmap/metadata.\n *\n * Reference: docs/notes/bf-3k90-compression-resume-solution-evaluation.md (Option B)\n *            docs/notes/bf-2vke-compression-resume-t4-reap-interaction.md\n *\n * @param meta - Beacon metadata to encode\n * @returns Uint8Array of encoded beacon with CRC-32\n */\nexport function encodeBeacon(meta: BeaconMeta): Uint8Array {\n  // Validate wire version and fragmentLen before encoding\n  if (meta.wireVersion !== WIRE_VERSION) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Cannot encode beacon for wire version ${meta.wireVersion}, this implementation is ${WIRE_VERSION}`,\n      {requestedVersion: meta.wireVersion, supportedVersion: WIRE_VERSION}\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (meta.fragmentLen !== L) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-VERSION',\n      `Cannot encode beacon with fragmentLen ${meta.fragmentLen}, wire constant is ${L}`,\n      {requestedL: meta.fragmentLen, wireConstantL: L, wireVersion: meta.wireVersion}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // bf-4bi6: Validate compression/resume conflict before any state changes\n  // This check prevents the unsafe combination where compression is enabled\n  // but resume is not disabled, which would cause silent corruption on sender restart.\n  //\n  // ## T4 Privacy Compliance Preservation (plan.md §12 T4b, E11)\n  // This conflict check PROTECTS T4 cleanup by preventing unsafe states before they exist.\n  // The check throws BEFORE any files are created, so cleanup is never needed for conflict cases.\n  // Normal flows (compression-only with ResumeDisabled, or resume-only) still trigger cleanup\n  // via export.ts (post-export deletion) and init.ts (startup reap of orphans).\n  // See: test/bf-1i2b-conflict-prevents-cleanup.test.ts for safety verification.\n  const compressionEnabled = (meta.flags & BeaconFlags.Compressed) !== 0;\n  const resumeDisabled = (meta.flags & BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled) !== 0;\n\n  if (compressionEnabled && !resumeDisabled) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-COMPRESSION-RESUME-CONFLICT',\n      `E-COMPRESSION-RESUME-CONFLICT: Compression cannot be enabled without disabling resume. ` +\n      `When BeaconFlags.Compressed is set, BeaconFlags.ResumeDisabled must also be set. ` +\n      `This incompatibility is required because CompressionStream offers no determinism guarantee across ` +\n      `browser restarts, making resume unsafe (see bf-17s0, bf-2w1a).`,\n      {\n        flags: meta.flags,\n        compressionEnabled,\n        resumeDisabled,\n      }\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Calculate total size\n  const filenameBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(meta.filename);\n  const mimeTypeBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(meta.mimeType);\n\n  if (filenameBytes.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `Filename too long: ${filenameBytes.length} bytes (max ${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES})`,\n      {length: filenameBytes.length, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_FILENAME_BYTES}\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (mimeTypeBytes.length > BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES) {\n    throw new BeaconValidationError(\n      'E-META-BOUNDS',\n      `MIME type too long: ${mimeTypeBytes.length} bytes (max ${BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES})`,\n      {length: mimeTypeBytes.length, max: BEACON_LIMITS.MAX_MIMETYPE_BYTES}\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Fixed fields: streamId(4) + wireVersion(1) + originalSize(6) + payloadLen(6) +\n  //              blockSize(3) + blockCount(3) + fragmentLen(2) + degreeCap(1) +\n  //              flags(1) + blockHashLen(1) + wholeFileHash(32) + manifestHash(4) = 64 bytes\n  // Variable: filenameLen(1) + filename + mimeTypeLen(1) + mimeType\n  // CRC-32: 4 bytes\n  const fixedSize = 64;\n  const variableSize = 2 + filenameBytes.length + mimeTypeBytes.length;\n  const crcSize = 4;\n  const totalSize = fixedSize + variableSize + crcSize;\n\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(totalSize);\n  let offset = 0;\n\n  // Write fixed fields\n  const writeU32 = (value: number) => {\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 24) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 16) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 8) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = value & 0xff;\n  };\n\n  const writeU24 = (value: number) => {\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 16) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 8) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = value & 0xff;\n  };\n\n  const writeU16 = (value: number) => {\n    bytes[offset++] = (value >>> 8) & 0xff;\n    bytes[offset++] = value & 0xff;\n  };\n\n  const writeU8 = (value: number) => {\n    bytes[offset++] = value & 0xff;\n  };\n\n  // Fixed fields\n  writeU32(meta.streamId);\n  writeU8(meta.wireVersion);\n\n  // originalSize: 6 bytes, 48-bit (original uncompressed file size)\n  writeU8((meta.originalSize >>> 40) & 0xff);       // Byte 0 (MSB)\n  writeU32((meta.originalSize >>> 8) & 0xffffffff); // Bytes 1-4\n  writeU8(meta.originalSize & 0xff);                // Byte 5 (LSB)\n\n  // payloadLen: 6 bytes, 48-bit (actual payload length after compression)\n  writeU8((meta.payloadLen >>> 40) & 0xff);       // Byte 0 (MSB)\n  writeU32((meta.payloadLen >>> 8) & 0xffffffff); // Bytes 1-4\n  writeU8(meta.payloadLen & 0xff);                // Byte 5 (LSB)\n\n  writeU24(meta.blockSize);\n  writeU24(meta.blockCount);\n  writeU16(meta.fragmentLen);\n  writeU8(meta.degreeCap);\n  writeU8(meta.flags);\n  writeU8(meta.blockHashLen);\n\n  // wholeFileHash: 32 bytes\n  bytes.set(meta.wholeFileHash, offset);\n  offset += 32;\n\n  // manifestHash: 4 bytes (CRC-32 of manifest)\n  bytes.set(meta.manifestHash, offset);\n  offset += 4;\n\n  // Variable fields\n  writeU8(filenameBytes.length);\n  bytes.set(filenameBytes, offset);\n  offset += filenameBytes.length;\n\n  writeU8(mimeTypeBytes.length);\n  bytes.set(mimeTypeBytes, offset);\n  offset += mimeTypeBytes.length;\n\n  // Calculate and write CRC-32 over everything except the CRC itself\n  const crcBodyEnd = offset;\n  const crcValue = crc32(bytes.subarray(0, crcBodyEnd));\n\n  writeU32(crcValue);\n\n  return bytes;\n}\n","numLines":823,"startLine":1,"totalLines":823}}}
